Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any computer_vision module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 317. The projective resolution has length at most 686 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to computer_vision structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 317/686 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Statement: Let the computer_vision structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 201. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 539 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 201 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the computer_vision structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 539. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
